# Add 65 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 65 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native turn-based wolf-pack survival strategy game for the Commodore C64, Plus/4 and Commander CX16. C gameplay implements prey spawning and movement, wolf selection, energy costs, catching prey, scoring and ten levels, with separate machine I/O implementations.
- https://github.com/steph72/wolves/blob/af699693850f0b114978bf5dd718c0c8b7cc271b/Makefile
- https://github.com/steph72/wolves/blob/af699693850f0b114978bf5dd718c0c8b7cc271b/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Native 6502 graphical adventure engine and playable technology demo for Acorn Electron/BBC Micro Model B, inspired by the style of Sierra AGI. Includes a custom script bytecode VM, synonym-aware text parser, tiled backgrounds, sprites and compressed tape loading; this is an original engine, not a recovered AGI interpreter.
- https://github.com/jhhoward/ElectronAdventure/blob/e8762bb05243bbf1108c2d7df2faea3390c0063c/build.bat
- https://github.com/jhhoward/ElectronAdventure/blob/e8762bb05243bbf1108c2d7df2faea3390c0063c/README.md

- https://github.com/jhhoward/ElectronAdventure/tree/e8762bb05243bbf1108c2d7df2faea3390c0063c

### Classification (reviewed)

Complete VM implementation handles arithmetic, comparisons, jumps, parser-token matching, messages, room changes and object coordinates. Useful original engine plus supporting development pipeline. No root licence or COPYING file found in the pinned recursive tree. Public source availability does not establish redistribution rights.

### Identity (reviewed)

Large native MSX multiplayer snake game with up to eight players, battle/training game states, forty levels, score codes, sound and joystick-adapter support. Implemented in C using MSXgl, with substantial game code and data rather than an engine-only sample.

### Identity (reviewed)

Substantial native MSX adventure/action game source with a banked overworld, title sequence, scrolling tile renderer, sprites, collision system, inventory/weapons, NPCs and enemy entities. C implementation coordinates segmented modules and interrupt-driven display updates.

### Identity (reviewed)

Historical original MSX Asteroids-like game by Eric Boon and Manuel Bilderbeek, preserved as C source with CVS-era revision headers. Contains ship/asteroid/UFO objects, bullets, explosions, collision detection, scoring, shield-energy and level/bonus handling.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native MSX turbo R raycasting demo game by Wouter Vermaelen and Artrag, also released as ANGRYBOT.COM. Implements fixed-point 64-column ray casting, textured/flat drawing, sprite rendering, movement clipping, dungeon loading and NPC behavior in C with performance-critical Z80 assembly.
- https://github.com/artrag/mz3d2/blob/c7c1af26213e19c7e336382cb0ac841ebfd0bcdb/LICENSE

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Amstrad CPC 464 runner by The CiAO Project, written in Z80 assembly with CPCTelera. Separate managers/systems implement scrolling grassfield, levels, player/enemy entities, AI control, physics, collisions, sound, menus, death and victory/restart flow.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native CPCRetroDev 2019 Amstrad CPC platform game, titled Super Tongueboy in README and Super Tongue Dino in source/configuration. Assembly entity system includes level/obstacle managers, invisibility, player input, enemy behavior, physics, collisions, rendering, deaths and in-game menus.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Amstrad CPC 464 roguelike/dungeon-action shooter in Z80 assembly using CPCTelera. Game loop generates levels and rooms, updates player/enemies/attacks, checks bullet, stairs, doors, object and actor collisions, and handles room changes, HUD and game-over state.

Original CPC RetroDev 2016 Amstrad action game source dump with player platform movement, enemy behaviors, collisions, level data, graphics, sound and interrupt routines. Includes a coherent main assembly unit with included subsystems and level/enemy initialization.

Native Amstrad CPC two-player arena-action game from SubXplosion’s 2016 academic project. Large C implementation handles characters Jimmy/Obdulia, controls, movement/collisions, ammunition, shots, keys/doors, arena blocks, rounds, score, menus and music.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Amstrad CPC zoo-themed action/collection game in C. The source menu calls it Zoo Clean (and retains Scape text), while repository is zoo-care. Gameplay includes directional movement, bait/attack controls, guard/enemy state behavior, collectibles, cage-opening objectives, lives and level changes.
- https://github.com/b-suarez/zoo-care/blob/fd455f23f51a44e420bdb777338ea875c9e6c197/Makefile

### Identity (reviewed)

Original work-in-progress Sierra-style point-and-click adventure for Amiga. Native C includes room loading, text handling, images, custom screen/UI abstraction and a separate vocabulary/room-data compiler. Distinct title from commercial Murder! and from the author's Parrot project.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original solid-polygon space-trading/combat prototype inspired by Frontier: Elite II. Contains substantial original universe generation, terrain renderer, physics, ports, contracts, combat, save state and ECS/AGA/RTG Amiga backends, rather than an emulated original game.
- https://github.com/sacredbanana/astralbound/blob/07829b8c78f1ece3b75ab4460115ca3f351d0cc5/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved original 1993 Amiga game source by Paul Grebenc, uploaded by its author. Full C game loop includes thrust, collision, landing pads, scoring, BOB graphics, keyboard/audio and memory services. This is an independent Amiga implementation, not the indexed C64 Jupiter Lander disassembly.
- https://github.com/pg94au/JupiterLander/blob/b4938b1e30b6fbd080bb4a812c37f1c64b6af2fc/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Compact native custom-screen Amiga game launcher experiment by MrZammler. C source implements manifest/game-list display, gameport joystick navigation and Execute-based launch, with a classic VBCC build. Distinct lightweight interface from full iGame.
- https://github.com/MrZammler/iGameLite/blob/38ea2a56b0ffa2558337c6b6f13476751a0a7af8/igamelite.c

### Identity (reviewed)

From-scratch C++20 recreation of John Calhoun's Macintosh Glider 4 with original house-file compatibility and a separate house editor. Implements its own physics/game state, object behavior, binary house reader/writer, SDL renderer and audio. Independent of Aerofoil, which ports Glider PRO.
- https://github.com/marcusspangenberg/glider/blob/4caacc11515d9160000bdbd84c802b6b330e7114/README.md
- https://github.com/marcusspangenberg/glider/blob/4caacc11515d9160000bdbd84c802b6b330e7114/src/Game.cpp
- https://github.com/marcusspangenberg/glider/blob/4caacc11515d9160000bdbd84c802b6b330e7114/src/HouseReader.cpp

Substantial complete game/editor source. Explicit rewrite and original-format compatibility distinguish this from asset-only fan projects; no emulator CPU wrapper. New code GPL-3.0 root LICENSE; original art/sounds/house files separately credited under MIT in README and THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original RISC OS Star Trek-themed spaceship/shooter learning project. Tokenised BASIC application files are accompanied by readable plain-text .bas equivalents with ship movement, shields, phasers, enemies, projectiles, sprites and audio.
- https://github.com/stevenhowes/TheEscape/blob/5c9403087819639176eb0cbf3ea343445c0b945b/README.md

Substantial BASIC gameplay implementation, explicitly a learning exercise; description says discontinued/replaced by CTheEscape, not evidence that successor is contained here. GPL-3.0 code license. Star Trek trademarks/copyright remain respective owners; no broad asset-rights claim.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native AmigaOS package manager with CLI, GadTools and MUI interfaces. Portable C core implements signed catalog verification, dependency resolution, recipes and receipts; Amiga layer implements HTTP/AmiSSL downloads and DOS-library install/remove operations. Independent package-manager component extracted from Amiga Imager.
- https://github.com/thomas-luebker/amipkg/blob/66cfb9888f7d6b28d1411ff254817ee14ed4e043/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantial install/dependency/security implementation. install.c explicitly says hardware-unverified, so upstream marketing does not establish operational safety or tested installation. Apache-2.0 main code; vendored MUI/AmiSSL headers and TweetNaCl keep their own notices/licenses.

### Identity (reviewed)

ANSI C AmigaOS game-development library implementing screens, Copper synchronization, IFF loading, icons and graphics.library-compatible BOB/VSprites with custom accelerated blitter/mask operations. Substantial reusable graphics subsystem rather than the author's skeletal Blaster game.
- https://github.com/68kPoker/GameX-Gels/blob/dcb6cd9aca0c7b88258a4bfd8a0fedbad176a525/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Commodore 64 implementation based on Amiga Nuclear Reaction. C/6502 assembly provides atom-placement chain reactions, two-player turns, winner detection, AI search, PETSCII rendering, SID sound and hardware input. Substantial independent game implementation.

### Identity (reviewed)

Pascal/Foxy native Mega Drive assembly port of ERE's Atari ST poker game. README explicitly credits Foxy's reverse engineering; source implements card/deal evaluation, betting turns, opponent decisions and Sega display/input/audio routines.

### Identity (reviewed)

Educational native Atari ST C/assembly Flappy Bird recreation with complete staged implementations. Latest stage includes game state/collision, monochrome raster routines, menu, score, PSG effects/music, IKBD input, vectors and VBL timing. Distinct from deferred BBC/Electron binary-only Flappy Bird release.
- https://github.com/Mohammed-Arab/Flappy-Bird/blob/7682f8926e08ab8c85b2978ddc4b7396f8618f0c/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Tomasz Słanina’s 2007 native WonderSwan Color Snake game, published with its complete 80186-compatible assembly, level data and a C COM-to-cartridge converter. This is original homebrew rather than recovered commercial source.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original Vectrex homebrew by Brian Jones. A single-cell organism orbits nuclei, jumps between them and tethers the board while avoiding biological hazards; the repository exposes the native game implementation rather than wrapping an old ROM.
- https://github.com/bjonesdeft/vectrex-paramecium/blob/75492ed4303965e49b24a6ce9608b4e0389cbedb/src/main.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed 2,467-line C implementation, fixed-point orbit lookup tables, board geometry, hazards and native drawing budget choices; README describes 50 levels and beta .07. README only gives © 2026 Brian Jones and boot-ROM mark PROK. No redistributable/open-source grant found; source-visible, rights uncertain.

### Identity (reviewed)

Oscar Toledo’s original Zombie Near action game source release, preserving dated development snapshots and native platform versions. The ColecoVision game contains its own Z80 game loop, enemy AI, maps and SN76489 music rather than emulating an existing game.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native NGPC scrolling-shooter demo built on NGPC Template 2026. Unlike a bare starter template, it contains a substantial distinct shooter implementation, scripted stages, multiple enemy patterns, bosses, graphics and audio.

### Identity (reviewed)

WonderSwan Bad Apple demo with a native banked command-stream/tile video player and interrupt-driven PCM/IMA-ADPCM audio, plus a Java video quantizer. It is native media-player homebrew, not a recovered game executable.
- https://github.com/asiekierka/bad-apple-for-wswan/blob/c39dca7100208d22cb309e51149b54236d5eec58/src/main.c
- https://github.com/asiekierka/bad-apple-for-wswan/blob/c39dca7100208d22cb309e51149b54236d5eec58/src/audio.S

- https://github.com/asiekierka/bad-apple-for-wswan/blob/c39dca7100208d22cb309e51149b54236d5eec58/README.md
- https://github.com/asiekierka/bad-apple-for-wswan/commit/c39dca7100208d22cb309e51149b54236d5eec58

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed command decoder with tile/bank/frame operations and native interrupt ADPCM handler; this is real playback implementation. Quantizer is a supporting asset pipeline. WonderSwan C and ASM files embed MIT notices; latest commit explicitly licenses WS code as MIT. No blanket license for third-party Bad Apple music/video or all Java tooling is inferred.

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent native replacement for the FreyaBIOS/FreyaOS WonderWitch abstraction layer. It aims to make existing freely available WonderWitch software run with third-party WonderSwan cartridges and emulators, with BIOS services, interrupt plumbing and OS/filesystem code.
- https://github.com/OpenWitch/AthenaOS/blob/d37beae7482616313883dcfa4bdb7114d1ef5749/bios/main.s
- https://github.com/OpenWitch/AthenaOS/blob/d37beae7482616313883dcfa4bdb7114d1ef5749/bios/disp/sprite_set_data.s
- https://github.com/OpenWitch/AthenaOS/blob/d37beae7482616313883dcfa4bdb7114d1ef5749/os/main.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed real-mode i186 BIOS entry/stack hardware setup, BIOS service modules and OS kernel filesystem table. Completeness and compatibility not independently tested. MIT for AthenaBIOS/AthenaOS. README identifies public-domain unscii and permissively licensed Misaki font provenance.

### Identity (reviewed)

Oscar Toledo’s CP1610-native replacement BASIC interpreter for the Intellivision Entertainment Computer System. It implements a full-screen editor, tokenized statements, floating-point expressions, graphics, sound and tape I/O.
- https://github.com/nanochess/ecsbasic/blob/934542dc6c25ad2c7dd2bc8ae73d15f613ae739f/basic.asm

### Identity (reviewed)

BASIC-to-CP1610-assembly cross-compiler with its own parser, intermediate-code representation, register tracking, peephole optimizer and native runtime prologue/epilogue. Provides a substantial original retro language toolchain rather than only editor support.
- https://github.com/nanochess/IntyBASIC/blob/9393a28e4d3384c67b8616d6dbbda4443fb83673/IntyBASIC.cpp

### Identity (reviewed)

Pre-alpha collection of reusable 6809 routines/macros for Vectrex vector drawing, timers, scoring, text, raw audio and DS2431 EEPROM. It adapts existing routines and some BIOS calls into macros to trade ROM space for cycles.
- https://github.com/gauze/LibVec/blob/23e668cc25c5e74efbf8696d46d3bb17721733b8/lib/drawing/drawing-macros.i
- https://github.com/gauze/LibVec/blob/23e668cc25c5e74efbf8696d46d3bb17721733b8/lib/general/clock.i

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed actual VIA-register vector drawing macro, frame-counting clock and raw sound routines. README explicitly says version 0.01 pre-alpha and acknowledges adapted code. No root license or clear reuse grant found. README credits Malban Vide, Chris Malcolm and Graham Toal; provenance/permission of individual borrowed routines remains uncertain.

### Identity (reviewed)

Extended pcmenc audio conversion toolkit based on Arturo Ragozini/Daniel Vik’s MSX encoder, adding SN76489 targets, packing methods, banking and cycle-budgeted Z80 replay routines. Actual encoder and native players are present.
- https://github.com/maxim-zhao/pcmenc/blob/1b6784396b8c5e138931d02dc81a20bf82c204c8/encoder/pcmenc.cpp
- https://github.com/maxim-zhao/pcmenc/blob/1b6784396b8c5e138931d02dc81a20bf82c204c8/encoder/Makefile
- https://github.com/maxim-zhao/pcmenc/blob/1b6784396b8c5e138931d02dc81a20bf82c204c8/encoder/resample.c
- https://github.com/maxim-zhao/pcmenc/blob/1b6784396b8c5e138931d02dc81a20bf82c204c8/players/sms/replayer_core_p4_rto3_44010Hz.asm
- https://github.com/maxim-zhao/pcmenc/blob/1b6784396b8c5e138931d02dc81a20bf82c204c8/README.md
- https://github.com/maxim-zhao/pcmenc/commit/1b6784396b8c5e138931d02dc81a20bf82c204c8

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed 1,428-line WAV resampling/encoding implementation and matching cycle-timed SMS player collection. More chips/platforms are advertised, but native checked-in replayer targets are Z80 MSX/SMS. Main encoder header has zlib-style permission, original Ragozini/Vik credits and Maxim modifications. resample.c carries separate permissive historical notices; no blanket license inferred for every vendored component.

### Identity (reviewed)

Unofficial retranslation/re-localisation of the original Japanese Master System Phantasy Star. The source includes annotated Z80 disassembly, text/graphics reimplementation, grammar-aware multilingual scripts and gameplay/bug-fix patches, not merely released IPS/BPS binaries.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable C89 emulation library for Fairchild F8-family systems, including Channel F. Models the CPU instruction/ROMC machinery and memory, display, beeper, controllers and auxiliary devices; shared by multiple distinct platform frontends.
- https://github.com/celerizer/libpressf/blob/8116b72819aa84be233bfea98a6a513d46fbc124/src/emu.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Native PC-98 DOS agent plus Windows Python CLI and MCP server for bounded hardware/DOS inspection and file/directory transfer over RS-232C. Substantial command parser, DOS memory/device inspection and streaming transfer code; not a shell or game wrapper.

### Identity (reviewed)

Windows .NET input window and native FreeDOS(98) resident program transmit committed Japanese input into PC-98 BIOS keyboard queues. Contains serial protocol, TSR hooks, IRQ buffering and a device-driver alternative.
- https://github.com/saitamanistan/PC98IMEBridge/blob/480bdad0dfa9f9807afe630b1a3f26aeccb507f6/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable NEC PC-9801/PC-9821 emulator implementing V30/x86 instruction execution, machine bus, disk controllers, GDC/GRCG, YM2608 and compatible BIOS/DOS interception in C#. Not tied to one game.
- https://github.com/keijidoi/pc98/blob/21b1d59ec8f204f9f2a70344291ab173c03f6223/README.md

Actual instruction dispatch, register model and DOS memory/file handlers inspected. Author explicitly documents simplified interrupt timing, incomplete V30 instructions/EGC, limited ADPCM and serial/printer stubs. No standard reuse grant found; README says personal learning/research purpose. Public source does not establish open-source licensing.

### Identity (reviewed)

Host-side wxPython application with actual D88/FDI/HDI/HDM/raw/NHD parsing, FAT12/FAT16 filesystem access, PC-98 IPL/MBR partition detection, hex viewing and controlled writeback. Reusable preservation and inspection tooling.
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/98Bridge/blob/987ab6df3a047bd0d47297dee6dbfe9f311b17b7/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Rust/wxDragon GUI for detecting and analysing PKLite-packed DOS executables, with a Rust-port bitstream/Huffman/LZ decoder and experimental MZ output reconstruction. Substantial implementation exists, but executable restoration is incomplete.
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/README.md
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/src/pklite.rs
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/src/main.rs
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/Cargo.toml
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/LICENSE
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/commit/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f

### Classification (reviewed)

Important source/README discrepancy: detect_pklite requires an MZ header, so generic COM support claimed by README is not established. Output construction sets num_relocs=0, creates a fresh 32-byte header and does not append overlays; reliable relocation/overlay preservation is not implemented in inspected path. Treat as experimental analysis/unpacking tooling, not verified faithful restoration. Extra mode also carries documented approximation. MIT with explicit third-party attribution.
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/README.md
- https://github.com/NullMagic2/UnPKd/blob/73713a53404bb693f6ca4ff695e36fb1b40b5c9f/src/pklite.rs

### Identity (reviewed)

Complete native DOS tank-combat Pascal program with keyboard/mouse movement, projectiles, collision logic, scores and BGI graphics; original source and data/binary packaging remain available.
- https://github.com/sakinijino/Tank/blob/5a52b882eadc3bb17276d2a47c0690cbdd0c4b00/README

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected collision/projectile gameplay implementation. BGI drivers and GRAPH.TPU are bundled binary dependencies; no reproducibility claim. MIT grant names Zhao Qi, Duan Ran and Zhong Shan.

### Identity (reviewed)

Shaun Brandt/Holy Meatgoat Software collection of native DOS projects including Damanoid, Move-it Man, LOCGAED CGA sprite editor and a historical Allegro Tetris. Retained as one repository-level collection, not inflated into one row per directory.
- https://github.com/Damaniel/dos_games/blob/ba2a02fc38b347bb6e75b94e31a0f6a257f045d5/README.md

Actual Damanoid collision/dirty-tile logic inspected; LOCGAED/MOVEIT source present. README distinguishes complete/playable tools from early prototypes. ### Relationships (reviewed)

Independent homebrew adaptations of Arkanoid/Sokoban/Tetris ideas; no duplicate or lineage match established. LOCGAED and MOVEIT explicitly use Jason Knight’s public-domain CGA library associated with Paku Paku; this shared graphics dependency does not make the original games/editor duplicates.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Turbo Pascal 7 multimedia/game engine with VGA renderer, widget UI, TMX/PCX/BMP loading, resource manager, XMS, AdLib HSC music, Sound Blaster audio and example XiClone game.
- https://github.com/DynartInteractive/DOS-Game-Engine/blob/e12ec89185c683a69b8aa3f0fede05fbb8f2c6a2/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Read clipping and checkerboard fill implementation; extensive native subsystem code and test programs are present. Modern native homebrew, not recovered commercial engine. MIT code; DATA/BG.PCX, PLAYER.PCX, TILESET.PCX/PNG have separate noncommercial/open-source-use-with-credit terms and prohibit commercial use without a licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Pascal/x86 assembly VGA toolkit covering buffered blitting, BMP images, fonts, keyboard/mouse/joystick, Sound Blaster WAV playback, PC speaker, timer and package I/O, with sample chess, snake and Tetris games.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original 2020 game-jam pseudo-3D space shoot-em-up using vanilla DOS DIV Games Studio 2 and mode-7-style presentation. PRG game source and custom Watcom C++ DLL modules are included.

Inspected native game process/timer logic. Archived unfinished game; repository language override says Pascal but actual main program is DIV language, so not classified as Pascal. MIT licence for repository code, copyright José Miguel Sánchez Fernández (2020). README says 3D source assets came from Unity Asset Store; source exporter not included and asset rights require separate consideration.

### Identity (reviewed)

TypeScript browser reimplementation of Ben Freeman’s 1992 DOS BBS door game, retaining original Turbo Pascal source and ANSI art. Implements game-state flow, combat, progression, saving and terminal presentation without emulating a CPU.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reconstructs the Pascal compiler included in the Dubna monitor from a recovered object module. Includes disassembly, annotated recovery material, Pascal auxiliaries and a direct C++ translation that emits big-endian BESM-6 object modules.
- https://github.com/besm6/pascal-re/blob/a8b96b5fb34aef474f160bd3f33c2c6387d2a025/comp.sh

### Classification (reviewed)

Recovered object is selected by matching compiler build date; README explains symbol-table reconstruction and successful historical reassembly. C++ frontend consumes ASCII/UTF-8 and outputs BESM object bytes. MIT license in repository; historical compiler recovery scope should be distinguished from newly written tooling.

### Identity (reviewed)

Recovers Pascal-Autocode version 9 (17 May 1988), descended from the first BESM-6 Pascal compiler begun in 1975. The implementation emits Cyrillic-mnemonic autocode for the BESM-6 assembler and includes editor and formatting tools.
- https://github.com/besm6/pasauto-re/blob/f28889b7e0bdd23c320e44884b46ed1a8b6490a8/pascomp.sh

- https://github.com/besm6/pasauto-re/commit/f28889b7e0bdd23c320e44884b46ed1a8b6490a8

### Classification (reviewed)

Large typed Pascal compiler with parser/type structures and support programs; README details runtime overlay and autocode generation model. No explicit license file or grant found in pinned tree/README; public source availability is not an open-source license.

### Identity (reviewed)

Richard Cornwell's Smalltalk-80 clone based on the Blue Book, with a simplified/custom VM, C bootstrap compiler, native Smalltalk compiler, object memory, image snapshots and a partly finished graphical interface.
- https://github.com/rcornwell/smalltalk/blob/2fe8399c1e08afe24704d7bb5734c1f82b56688f/README.md
- https://github.com/rcornwell/smalltalk/blob/2fe8399c1e08afe24704d7bb5734c1f82b56688f/LICENSE.txt

- https://github.com/rcornwell/smalltalk/commit/2fe8399c1e08afe24704d7bb5734c1f82b56688f

### Classification (reviewed)

Independent from dbanay/Smalltalk: custom opcode encoding and self-bootstrap. README says graphics incomplete and debugging code untested; 64-bit object memory requires changes. Artistic License 2.0 in LICENSE.txt and implementation headers.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserves IBM 709 diagnostics covering CPU, indexing, floating point, memory, tape, cards, drums, printer, display and special controls. Includes dated assembly listings, scan images, loader/diagnostic assembler sources and card/tape generation tools.

### Identity (reviewed)

Cornwell's 1990s X Toolkit widget set developed alongside XMS mail, addressing Athena widget limitations through cascaded menus, selection controls, cached 3D drawing and an extensible widget library.

### Identity (reviewed)

Leonid Broukhis and Serge Vakulenko's Unix for BK-0010/BK-0011M, derived from revived LSX and Mini-Unix V6 sources. Modernizes ancient C, fixes cross-compilers and relocates the kernel into the BK memory map.
- https://github.com/sergev/bkunix/blob/9954fb39fbf10682bddcd0f05ca7e046c56a602d/README.md

Modernizes Ken Thompson's SNO compiler/interpreter from the Unix 4 tape. Retains legacy C sources alongside a C++17 implementation and tests, documenting deviations from standard Snobol III.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserves Steve Hosgood and Terry Barnaby's early-1980s cut-down Unix-like OMU kernel, originally for a homemade 6809 computer and ported to 68000 with multitasking, hard-disk support and UID/GID handling.
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/omu09/omu09/src/makefile
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/omu09/omu09/src/sys.c
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/COPYING
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/README
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/tree/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/commit/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual 1984 startup/syscall implementation present. README cautions original compiler used signed 16-bit int, signed 8-bit char and even-aligned multibyte structures. GPLv2 COPYING with kernel/syscall clarification, credited to Hosgood and Barnaby.
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/omu09/omu09/src/makefile
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/omu09/omu09/src/sys.c
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/blob/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131/COPYING

- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/tree/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU/commit/e986aa90493e849f50f35a24356b786076ab3131

### Source CPU (reviewed)

README explicitly identifies the 6809 original as the predecessor of the included 68000 port; not inferred from the modern archive host.

### Identity (reviewed)

Hosts a cleaned-up version of Dave Dunfield's historic Micro-C C-subset compiler, optimizer, preprocessor, libraries and target code generators, aimed at bootstrapping on a modern host.
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/blob/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48/MCC/Makefile
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/blob/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48/README.md
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/blob/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48/MCC/compile.c
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/blob/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48/LICENSE
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/blob/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48/COPY.TXT
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/tree/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/commit/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantial actual compiler parser/symbol/expression stacks and many codegen backends. Bootstrap still in progress; no end-to-end builds claimed. Derived cleanup GPLv3; COPY.TXT preserves Dunfield's original reasonable-purpose grant and attribution/source-location requests. Original archive remains separately available.
- https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC/blob/591a67748cab006d496b3e9736b3e856276c4c48/MCC/Makefile

    "source": "https://github.com/EtchedPixels/OMU"
  },
  {
    "id": "wide-20261018s-dunfield-micro-c-preserved-cleanup",
    "kind": "github-repository",

    "review_state": "substantially-reviewed",
    "source": "https://github.com/EtchedPixels/DunfieldMicroC"
  }
]

## Research event notes

Bounded broad discovery with programmatic URL screening before pinned implementation/build/licence inspection and title/lineage deduplication. Not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Source completeness, rights and AI attribution remain qualified. No upstream software executed; all earlier catalogue records preserved.
